\times\Big(\exp\{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}a\sinh(aT)[\tanh(aT)\sinh\vartheta+\cosh\vartheta]\}\\
-\exp\{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}L[\tanh(aT)\sinh\vartheta+\cosh\vartheta]\}\Big).

(B.80)

From this we see that these behave as oscillatory distributions around zero, and
on the limit
L\to\infty
we find the term
\exp\{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}L[\tanh(aT)\sinh\vartheta\pm\cosh\vartheta]\}
averages out to zero. In
mathematical notation

f_{\pm}(\vartheta,T,\infty)\coloneqq\lim_{L\to\infty}f_{\pm}(\vartheta,T,L)=-\frac{\mathrm{i}\exp\{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}a\sinh(aT)[\tanh(aT)\sinh\vartheta\mp\cosh\vartheta]\}}{k_{\perp}[\tanh(aT)\sinh\vartheta\mp\cosh\vartheta]}.

(B.81)

The same arguments can be used in the limit
T\to\infty
, as the hyperbolic
sine is a strictly increasing function, and thus

f_{\pm}(\vartheta,\infty,\infty)\coloneqq\lim_{T\to\infty}f_{\pm}(\vartheta,T,\infty)=0,

(B.82)

meaning there is no contribution from the inertial parts of the motion in the case the particle is accelerated an infinite amount of time, as we expected.

B.5
Angular integrals encountered when computing the full perturbation

We have the angular integrals

\displaystyle\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.83a)

\displaystyle\Xi_{2}(u,\varphi)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\sin\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.83b)

\displaystyle\Xi_{3}(u,\varphi)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.83c)

\displaystyle\Xi_{4}(u,\varphi)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\sin\vartheta\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.83d)

\displaystyle\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)=\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}(\cos^{2}\vartheta-\sin^{2}\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.83e)

and their complex conjugates. We can use Ref.
[
66
]
or software like Wolfram Mathematica for the first one, yielding

\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)=2\pi\mathrm{J}_{0}(u),

(B.84)

i.e., the Bessel function of the first kind and zero order. We can now note that
if we take the derivative with respect to
u
of this function, we find

\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)=\mathrm{i}\cos\varphi\,\Xi_{3}(u,\varphi)+\mathrm{i}\sin\varphi\,\Xi_{2}(u,\varphi)=-2\pi\mathrm{J}_{1}(u),

(B.85)

and the partial derivative with respect to
\varphi
yields

\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)=0\qquad\Longleftrightarrow\qquad\Xi_{2}(u,\varphi)\cos\varphi=\Xi_{3}(u,\varphi)\sin\varphi,

(B.86)

system that is satisfied if (these can also be verified directly with software like Wolfram Mathematica)

\displaystyle\Xi_{2}(u,\varphi)

\displaystyle=2\pi\mathrm{i}\sin\varphi\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(u),

\displaystyle\Xi_{3}(u,\varphi)

\displaystyle=2\pi\mathrm{i}\cos\varphi\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(u).

(B.87)

With these we can find the remaining two and we won’t need to derive with
respect to
\varphi
. First, we can note that we can rewrite
\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)
in two ways, using integrals we don’t know yet:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\cos^{2}\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}[\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)+\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)],

(B.88)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}u\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\sin^{2}\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}[\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)-\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)],

(B.89)

which allows us to write the partial derivatives of
\Xi_{2}
and
\Xi_{3}
with
respect to
u
as

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\Xi_{2}(u,\varphi)=\mathrm{i}\left(\Xi_{4}(u,\varphi)\cos\varphi+\frac{\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)-\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)}{2}\sin\varphi\right)=\mathrm{i}\pi[\mathrm{J}_{0}(u)-\mathrm{J}_{2}(u)]\sin\varphi,

(B.90)

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial u}\Xi_{3}(u,\varphi)=\mathrm{i}\left(\Xi_{4}(u,\varphi)\sin\varphi+\frac{\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)+\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)}{2}\cos\varphi\right)=\mathrm{i}\pi[\mathrm{J}_{0}(u)-\mathrm{J}_{2}(u)]\cos\varphi.

(B.91)

The terms that are dependent of
\Xi_{1}(u,\varphi)
vanish identically with the
terms that are written using
\mathrm{J}_{0}(u)
, leaving two unknowns and two
independent equations. Solving the system we find

\displaystyle\Xi_{4}(u,\varphi)

\displaystyle=-\pi\sin(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(u),

\displaystyle\Xi_{5}(u,\varphi)

\displaystyle=-2\pi\cos(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(u),

(B.92)

which are equivalent to the result of Eq. (
3.143
).

B.6
Explicit deductions of the components of the perturbation

We can write the other components of the perturbation in the same fashion we found Eq. (
3.146
).
For the
\eta x
component

\displaystyle RT_{\eta x}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa}{\sqrt{8\pi^{2}a}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathrm{d}^{2}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,\left(\mathrm{i}\,W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{\eta x}-\mathrm{i}\,\overline{W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{\eta x}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}}{16\pi^{2}a}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathrm{d}^{2}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\times\Bigg\{\mathrm{i}\left[-\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathrm{i}\left[-\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}\mathrm{i}}{8\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}k_{\perp}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg\{-\left[\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left[\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\cos\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{-\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\},

(B.93)

which can be reduced using the identities above

\displaystyle RT_{\eta x}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}\mathrm{i}}{16\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}k_{\perp}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg\{-\left[\bm{(}2\pi\mathrm{i}\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\cos\varphi\bm{)}\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left[\bm{(}\overline{2\pi\mathrm{i}\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\cos\varphi}\bm{)}\,\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}}{4\pi a}\cos\varphi\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}k_{\perp}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a),

(B.94)

and analogously (the calculation is almost the same)

RT_{\eta y}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}}{4\pi a}\sin\varphi\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}k_{\perp}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a).

(B.95)

Other components can also be easily obtained

\displaystyle RT_{xy}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa}{\sqrt{8\pi^{2}a}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathrm{d}^{2}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,\left(\mathrm{i}\,W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{xy}-\mathrm{i}\,\overline{W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{xy}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg[\mathrm{i}\,\left(\mathrm{i}\cos\vartheta\sin\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathrm{i}\,\left(\overline{\mathrm{i}\cos\vartheta\sin\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=-\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg[\left(\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\cos\vartheta\sin\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(\overline{\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\cos\vartheta\sin\vartheta\,\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=-\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Big[\left(-\pi\sin(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\left(-\pi\sin(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\overline{\,\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right)\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi a}\sin(2\varphi)\,\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,\left[\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)+\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\sin(2\varphi)\,\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a),

(B.96)

\displaystyle RT_{xx}=-RT_{yy}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa}{\sqrt{8\pi^{2}a}}\iint_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mathrm{d}^{2}\mathbf{k}_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,\left(\mathrm{i}\,W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{xx}-\mathrm{i}\,\overline{W^{(2,\mathrm{s},0\mathbf{k}_{\perp})}_{xx}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg\{\mathrm{i}\,\left[\mathrm{i}\left(\frac{\cos^{2}\vartheta-\sin^{2}\vartheta}{2}\right)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathrm{i}\,\left[-\mathrm{i}\,\overline{\left(\frac{\cos^{2}\vartheta-\sin^{2}\vartheta}{2}\right)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}}\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{16\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg\{\left[\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta(\cos^{2}\vartheta-\sin^{2}\vartheta)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\,\left[\overline{\int_{0}^{2\pi}\mathrm{d}\vartheta(\cos^{2}\vartheta-\sin^{2}\vartheta)\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}k_{\perp}x_{\perp}\cos(\vartheta-\varphi)}}\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=-\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{16\pi^{2}a}\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigg\{\left[-2\pi\cos(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\,\left[\overline{-2\pi\cos(2\varphi)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})}\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{8\pi a}\cos(2\varphi)\!\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\!\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\left[\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)+\overline{\mathrm{H}^{(2)}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\cos(2\varphi)\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{d}{k_{\perp}}\,k_{\perp}\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(k_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(k_{\perp}x_{\perp})\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(k_{\perp}/a)\,.

(B.97)

B.7
Computation of the integrals involving 3 of Bessel’s functions

The expressions we have to compute are of the type

\displaystyle M(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.98)

\displaystyle N(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta.

(B.99)

Only thing we will demand for now is that
\alpha>0
. The first one of these
can be evaluated with the assistance of the identity
[
51
]

\displaystyle\varTheta_{\mu\nu}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)

\displaystyle\coloneqq\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta^{\nu+1}\,\mathrm{K}_{\mu}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{I}_{\mu}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta

\displaystyle=\frac{(\alpha\beta)^{-\nu-1}\gamma^{\nu}\mathrm{e}^{-(\nu+1/2)\pi\mathrm{i}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}(\Theta^{2}-1)^{\nu/2+1/4}}\,\mathcal{D}_{\mu-1/2}^{\nu+1/2}(\Theta),

(B.100)

valid for
\mathrm{Re}\,\alpha>|\mathrm{Re}\,\beta|+|\mathrm{Im}\,\gamma|
(i.e., we can take
\mathrm{Re}\,\beta=\mathrm{Im}\,\gamma=0

to satisfy our needs),
\mathrm{Re}\,\nu>-1
, and
\mathrm{Re}(\nu+\mu)>-1
, where the value of
\Theta
is defined by

\Theta=\frac{\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}}{2\alpha\beta}

(B.101)

and

\mathcal{D}_{\nu}^{1/2}(\Theta)=\mathrm{i}\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}}(\Theta^{2}-1)^{-1/4}\left[\Theta+\sqrt{\Theta^{2}-1}\right]^{-\nu-1/2}.

(B.102)

Now, the integral of Eq. (
B.100
) can be transformed to the form
of Eq. (
B.98
) if we use the definition of the modified
Bessel functions of the first kind
[
61
]

\mathrm{I}_{\nu}(x)=\mathrm{i}^{-\nu}\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(\mathrm{i}x),

(B.103)

to see that

\varTheta_{20}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{I}_{2}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta\\
=\frac{(\alpha\beta)^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{-\pi\mathrm{i}/2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}(\Theta^{2}-1)^{1/4}}\,\mathcal{D}_{3/2}^{1/2}(\Theta)=-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\sqrt{2\pi}\alpha\beta(\Theta^{2}-1)^{1/4}}\,\mathcal{D}_{3/2}^{1/2}(\Theta)\\
=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(\alpha\vartheta)\,[\mathrm{i}^{-2}\mathrm{J}_{2}(\mathrm{i}\beta\vartheta)]\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta\\
=-\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{2}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{2}(\mathrm{i}\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{0}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta=-M(\alpha,\mathrm{i}\beta,\gamma).

(B.104)

Now, after a lot of simplification the integral (
B.98
) becomes

M(\alpha,\mathrm{i}\beta,\gamma)=-\frac{[\Theta-\sqrt{\Theta^{2}-1}]^{2}}{2\alpha\beta\sqrt{\Theta^{2}-1}},

(B.105)

then, if we restrict
\mathrm{Arg}\,\beta<\pi
[in order to have
\sqrt{(\beta^{2})}=\beta
] we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{\Theta^{2}-1}=\frac{\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}}{2\alpha\beta},

(B.106a)

\displaystyle[\Theta-\sqrt{\Theta^{2}-1}]^{2}=\frac{[\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}]^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}},

(B.106b)

from where it is straightforward to see that

M(\alpha,\mathrm{i}\beta,\gamma)=-\frac{[\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}]^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}},

(B.107)

a relatively manageable form. Now, we make the replacement
\beta\to\mathrm{i}\beta
(compatible with all our assumptions if we limit
\beta>0
from now on), from where we see that

M(\alpha,-\beta,\gamma)=\frac{[\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}]^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}},

(B.108)

and as the Bessel function of the first kind is even for even orders, in
particular
\mathrm{J}_{2}(x)=\mathrm{J}_{2}(-x)
, we have

M(\alpha,-\beta,\gamma)=M(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\frac{[\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}]^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}}.

(B.109)

We can also note that

\displaystyle\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\alpha^{4}+2\alpha^{2}(-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})+(-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\alpha^{4}+2\alpha^{2}(\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})+(\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\alpha^{4}+2\alpha^{2}(\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})+(\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}},

(B.110)

meaning we can also use the form

M(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\frac{[\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}]^{2}}{4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}}.

(B.111)

We can then find

\displaystyle M(a^{-1},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta},x_{\perp})=\frac{\left[a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2}-\sqrt{(a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}}\right]^{2}}{4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}\sqrt{(a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}}},

\displaystyle M(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta})=\frac{\left[a^{-2}+\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}-x_{\perp}^{2}-\sqrt{(a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}}\right]^{2}}{4a^{-2}x_{\perp}^{2}\sqrt{(a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}}}.

Recognizing the expression of Eq. (
2.21
) we find

\displaystyle M(a^{-1},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta},x_{\perp})=\frac{[a^{-2}-\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}+x_{\perp}^{2}-2a^{-1}\rho_{0}(x)]^{2}}{8a^{-5}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}\rho_{0}(x)},

(B.112)

\displaystyle M(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta})=\frac{[a^{-2}+\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}-x_{\perp}^{2}-2a^{-1}\rho_{0}(x)]^{2}}{8a^{-3}x_{\perp}^{2}\rho_{0}(x)}.

(B.113)

On the other hand, integral (
B.99
) cannot be explicitly
found in any table of integrals as is; it is obfuscated by our previous use of
identity (
3.139
). We can rewrite it as

\displaystyle N(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\left[\mathrm{K}_{0}(\alpha\vartheta)+\frac{2}{\alpha\vartheta}\mathrm{K}_{1}(\alpha\vartheta)\right]\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{0}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta}_{N_{1}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)}+\frac{2}{\alpha}\underbrace{\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathrm{K}_{1}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,}_{N_{2}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)}

(B.114)

with the advantage that integrals of both of these kinds are treated in a paper by

\Citeauthor
fabrikantComputationInfiniteIntegrals2003
[
82
]
, and also in renowned tables of integrals like

\Citeauthor
gradshteynTableIntegralsSeries2014
[
66
]
. Consulting these references we find

\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta\,\mathrm{K}_{0}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta=\frac{\ell_{-}^{\nu}}{\ell_{+}^{\nu}(\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2})},

(B.115)

for
\gamma>0,\ \mathrm{Re}\,\nu>-1,\ \mathrm{Re}\,\alpha>|\mathrm{Im}\,\beta|
, where we used the definition

\ell_{\pm}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\coloneqq\frac{1}{2}\left(\sqrt{\alpha^{2}+(\beta+\gamma)^{2}}\pm\sqrt{\alpha^{2}+(\beta-\gamma)^{2}}\right),

(B.116)

thus

N_{1}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\frac{\ell_{-}}{\ell_{+}(\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2})}.

(B.117)

For the second one we can use
[
66
]
:

\varUpsilon_{\mu\nu\rho}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\coloneqq\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta^{\nu-\mu-\rho+1}\,\mathrm{J}_{\mu}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{\nu}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{K}_{\rho}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta\\
=\frac{2^{1+\nu-\mu-\rho}}{\gamma^{\mu}\beta^{\nu}\alpha^{\rho}\Gamma(\mu-\nu+\rho)}\int_{0}^{\ell_{-}}\frac{\vartheta^{1+2\nu-2\rho}\,[(\ell_{-}^{2}-\vartheta^{2})(\ell_{+}^{2}-\vartheta^{2})]^{\mu-\nu+\rho-1}}{(\beta^{2}-\vartheta^{2})^{\mu-\nu}}\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta,

(B.118)

valid for
\mathrm{Re}\,\alpha>|\mathrm{Im}\,\beta|
,
\gamma>0
, and
\mu-\nu+\rho>0
. From here we see that

\varUpsilon_{111}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\int_{0}^{\infty}\vartheta^{1-1-1+1}\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\gamma\vartheta)\,\mathrm{J}_{1}(\beta\vartheta)\,\mathrm{K}_{1}(\alpha\vartheta)\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta=N_{2}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)\\
=\frac{2^{1+1-1-1}}{\gamma^{1}\beta^{1}\alpha^{1}\Gamma(1-1+1)}\int_{0}^{\ell_{-}}\frac{\vartheta^{1+2(1)-2(1)}\,[(\ell_{-}^{2}-\vartheta^{2})(\ell_{+}^{2}-\vartheta^{2})]^{1-1+1-1}}{(\beta^{2}-\vartheta^{2})^{1-1}}\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta\\
=\frac{1}{\alpha\beta\gamma}\int_{0}^{\ell_{-}}\vartheta\,\mathrm{d}\vartheta=\frac{\ell_{-}^{2}(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)}{2\alpha\beta\gamma},

(B.119)

to finally obtain

N(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)=\frac{\ell_{-}}{\ell_{+}(\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2})}+\frac{\ell_{-}^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\beta\gamma}=\frac{\ell_{-}^{2}}{\ell_{+}\ell_{-}(\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2})}+\frac{\ell_{-}^{2}}{\alpha^{2}\beta\gamma}.

(B.120)

We can explicitly show the form of some of these
first, we can check that

\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2}=\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\beta^{2}\gamma^{2}},

(B.121)

which is extremely useful to check for the symmetry in the exchange
\beta\leftrightarrow\gamma
, but we need a little different form. Notice we can
reorder using a little algebra to see that

\ell_{+}^{2}-\ell_{-}^{2}=\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}.

(B.122)

Other important products like

\ell_{+}\ell_{-}=\beta\gamma,

(B.123)

and,

\ell_{-}^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\left[\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}\right],

(B.124)

allow us to obtain the explicit form

\displaystyle N(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2}-\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}}{2\beta\gamma}

\displaystyle\qquad\times\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}}+\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}\right).

(B.125)

This implies

\displaystyle N(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta})

\displaystyle=\frac{a^{-2}+x_{\perp}^{2}+\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}/a^{2}-2\rho_{0}(x)/a}{2x_{\perp}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}/a}\left(\frac{1}{2\rho_{0}(x)/a}+a^{2}\right).

(B.126)

Applying these we have

\displaystyle RT_{\eta\eta}=RT_{\zeta\zeta}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}\,M(a^{-1},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta},x_{\perp}),

(B.127)

\displaystyle RT_{\eta x}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}\cos\varphi\,N(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}),

(B.128)

\displaystyle RT_{\eta y}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}\sin\varphi\,N(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}),

(B.129)

\displaystyle RT_{xy}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\sin(2\varphi)\,M(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}),

(B.130)

\displaystyle RT_{xx}=-RT_{yy}=\frac{m\kappa^{2}}{4\pi a}\cos(2\varphi)\,M(a^{-1},x_{\perp},a^{-1}\mathrm{e}^{a\eta}).

(B.131)

Now, using
\mathbf{x}_{\perp}=(x,y)=(x_{\perp}\cos\varphi,x_{\perp}\sin\varphi)
,
we see that

\displaystyle\cos\varphi

\displaystyle=\frac{x}{x_{\perp}},

\displaystyle\sin\varphi

\displaystyle=\frac{y}{x_{\perp}},

\displaystyle\sin(2\varphi)

\displaystyle=\frac{2xy}{x_{\perp}^{2}},

\displaystyle\cos(2\varphi)

\displaystyle=\frac{x^{2}-y^{2}}{x_{\perp}^{2}},

(B.132)

and also noting that

\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}-\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\gamma^{2}}=\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}-\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}+4\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}=\sqrt{(\alpha^{2}+\beta^{2}+\gamma^{2})^{2}-4\beta^{2}\gamma^{2}},

(B.133)

which implies that

\displaystyle 2a^{-1}\rho_{0}(x)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{(a^{-2}+a^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}-x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-2}x_{\perp}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{(a^{-2}-a^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}+4a^{-4}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{(a^{-2}+a^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}+x_{\perp}^{2})^{2}-4a^{-2}\mathrm{e}^{2a\eta}x_{\perp}^{2}},

(B.134)

and is all we need to find the form of Eqs. (
3.150
)
